Use when authoring or critiquing a Control, Risk, Component, or Persona title or description to check a proposed term against the canonical (NIST-first) vocabulary, replace an invented term with its established equivalent, generalize a product/protocol-specific name to its role, or confirm a term is already grounded. ([cosai-oasis/secure-ai-tooling](https://github.com/cosai-oasis/secure-ai-tooling/tree/HEAD/scripts/skills/classical-lexicon) · ★107)
- **classical-mechanics**: Classical mechanics from kinematics through rotational dynamics and oscillations. Covers 1D/2D/3D motion, Newton's three laws, free-body diagrams, friction, projectile motion, circular motion, rotational mechanics, gravitation and orbits, work-energy theorem, conservation of momentum, collisions, and simple harmonic motion. Use when analyzing forces, trajectories, orbital mechanics, or any problem where objects move under deterministic forces at non-relativistic speeds. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/physics/classical-mechanics) · ★71)
- **classical-mechanics**: Solve Newtonian mechanics problems: kinematics, forces, work-energy, momentum, collisions, friction, projectile motion, constraints, non-inertial frames. Use whenever the user asks about forces, motion, energy conservation, or momentum, even if they just say 'physics problem' about masses, ramps, pulleys, or projectiles. ([hamzabellouch/agent-skills](https://github.com/hamzabellouch/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Physics/Physics_Master_Library/classical-mechanics) · ★8)
- **classical-mechanics**: Formulate, solve, simulate, and verify classical mechanics problems using Newtonian, Lagrangian, Hamiltonian, conservation-law, oscillation, rotation, and central-force methods. Use for particles, rigid bodies, constrained systems, collisions, orbital motion, and small oscillations. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/classical-mechanics) · ★4)
- **classical-mechanics**: Newtonian dynamics and motion analysis ([ffsshhttiikk/opencode-agents-skills](https://github.com/ffsshhttiikk/opencode-agents-skills/tree/HEAD/classical-mechanics) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **classical-mechanics**: Newtonian mechanics including Lagrangian and Hamiltonian dynamics, central forces, rigid body motion, small oscillations, and chaos theory for physics applications. ([ffsshhttiikk/opencode-agents-skills](https://github.com/ffsshhttiikk/opencode-agents-skills/tree/HEAD/physics/classical-mechanics) · ★3 · 3 copies)

- **classics-cc01-golf-gti-mechanics**: Rebuild and verify the CC.01 Mechanics Lab (Golf GTI feature on informatico.ai) - front-axle torque split for an open differential, a brake-based XDS+ and a clutch-pack lock, lap times as average speeds where the track length is stated, and the quarter-car body-bounce and wheel-hop frequencies - using a standard-library Python verifier that checks the published English and Spanish pages and fails under planted defects. - **classics-cc02-sagrada-stereotomy**: Rebuild and verify the CC.02 Stereotomy & Mechanics Lab (La Sagrada Família feature on informatico.ai) — doubly-ruled one-sheet hyperboloid straightness, hanging catenary vs. parabola equilibrium & column tilt angle, double-twisted 6/8/10/12 star column Boolean cross-sections, post-tensioned stone panel decompression, and 1936 plaster-shard algebraic hyperboloid recovery vs. freeform drift — using a standard-library Python verifier that checks the published English and Spanish pages and exits non-zero under five planted defects. ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/classics/cc02))
- **classics-cc03-mini-jcw-mechanics**: Rebuild and verify the CC.03 Mechanics Lab (MINI to F56 John Cooper Works Hot Chili on informatico.ai) - steady-state axle lateral load transfer and tire load sensitivity, adiabatic single-stop front brake rotor temperature rise, Brands Hatch Indy (1.208 miles) lap speeds, and US SAE vs. Mexico/EU metric power-unit conversion - using a standard-library Python verifier that checks the published English and Spanish pages and fails under five planted physics defects. ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/classics/cc03))
- **classics-reading**: 克制型中国古典文献解读。当用户要求解读、翻译、赏析《道德经》《易经》《阴符经》《庄子》等先秦典籍或古文经典，或问"某句话怎么理解""帮我解释某章"时使用。核心是防止模型自由发挥、编造注疏：强制版本先行、标注出处、并列争议、区分"字面义/注疏义/个人推演"三层，并把"无定论/无出处"设为合法输出。用户要求"克制模式解读"时也应触发。 ([i-stack/ai-coding-kit](https://github.com/i-stack/ai-coding-kit/tree/HEAD/skills-engineering/classics-reading) · ★3)
- **classificacao-despesa**: Guia para classificar itens de contratações de TIC por natureza de despesa (categoria econômica, grupo, modalidade, elemento, subelemento) e orientar a busca de códigos CATMAT/CATSER. Use junto com o Integrante Administrativo (Fase Preparatória) e o Fiscal Administrativo (Gestão do Contrato), ou quando alguém perguntar em que elemento de despesa entra um bem ou serviço de TIC.
